- **precheck-and-premortem**: Use BEFORE claiming "this works / no problem / it's done / it'll be fine" OR before committing to a plan/build. Forces a theoretical PRE-CHECK (recall + read + reason against the laws) and a PRE-MORTEM (enumerate failure modes + plan the fix in advance) FIRST, then a CALIBRATED claim (VERIFIED vs EXPECTED-but-unverified). Prevents the flip-flop where you assert "no problem" then reverse later. Triggers on: about to say works/fine/no-problem/done/should-work, about to launch a build/experiment, "이론상 문제 없는지", "말이 자꾸 달라", planning a multi-step or expensive action. - **precision-verify**: PyPTO 算子精度工具对比法。使用 pypto.pass_verify_save 和 torch.save 保存中间结果到文件，然后使用对比工具分析。专注：插入检查点 → 运行测试 → 对比分析 → 定位首个失败点。触发：使用精度对比工具保存并对比中间结果、定位首个失败点时使用。 ([hicann/cannbot-skills](https://github.com/hicann/cannbot-skills/tree/HEAD/ops/pypto-precision-compare/precision-verify) · ★18)
- **precision-verify**: PyPTO 算子精精度工具对比法。使用 pypto.pass_verify_save 和 torch.save 保存中间结果到文件，然后使用对比工具分析。专注：插入检查点 → 运行测试 → 对比分析 → 定位首个失败点。 当需要用 pass_verify_save/torch.save 保存中间结果并对比定位精度问题时使用此技能。 ([LeleCheung/Ascend-Optimization](https://github.com/LeleCheung/Ascend-Optimization/tree/HEAD/development/dependencies/sources/kernelgen/kb/sources/content/cannbot-skills/7fedd2ff5e1f/ops/pypto-precision-compare/precision-verify) · ★1 · 3 copies)

- **precision-voiceover-sync**: Aligns narration timing to on-screen actions so that clicks, reveals, and callouts land on the exact word or phrase describing them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/precursor-charge-state-filtering))
- **precursor-fragment-ion-pairing**: Use when you have raw LC-MS/MS data files (mzML/mzXML format from Thermo, Waters, or Bruker instruments) and a list of target compounds defined by precursor m/z values (and optionally retention time windows). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-fragment-ion-pairing) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-fragment-pairing-for-mrm**: Use when when designing a targeted lipidomics experiment and you have lipid species definitions (including chain composition and adducts) but need to configure precursor–fragment transitions for MRM or PRM acquisition on a Thermo QExactive HF, Agilent QTOF, or compatible high-resolution or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-fragment-pairing-for-mrm) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-interference-qc-score-calculation**: Use when quantifying iTRAQ, TMT (up to 11-plex), or TMTpro (up to 16-plex) labeled peptides from mzML files plus PSM hit files and you need QC scores (ms1PrecSignal, peptideIntensityScore, msnTotalSignal) to filter compression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/precursor-interference-qc-score-calculation))
- **precursor-ion-chromatogram-extraction**: Use when you have raw DIA mass spectrometry data files (.raw, .d, or .wiff formats) from timsTOF, TripleTOF, or Orbitrap instruments and need to extract precursor ion chromatograms for quality assessment or downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-ion-chromatogram-extraction) · 3 copies)
- **precursor-ion-information-parsing**: Use when an mzXML (or other supported peak-list/XML-based) MS file must be parsed in Java to recover scan records with precursor ion details and peak arrays for downstream proteomics analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/precursor-ion-information-parsing))
- **precursor-ion-isolation-windowing**: Use when processing SWATH-MS (Sequential Windowed Acquisition of all Theoretical Mass-spectra) raw data files (mzML or vendor format) for untargeted metabolomics, specifically before attempting to deconvolute overlapping MS/MS spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-ion-isolation-windowing) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-ion-mass-error-calculation**: Use when performing omics-agnostic MS quality control on raw MS runs (LC-MS, LC-IMS-MS, DDA/DIA, or direct infusion) and you need per-ion error metrics (mass, RT, AT/1/K0) plus spectral counts and TIC to detect instrument drift or outliers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-ion-mass-error-calculation))
- **precursor-ion-metadata-extraction**: Use when mzML files must be parsed in Java to recover per-spectrum precursor m/z and charge from spectrum metadata, alongside peak m/z and intensity arrays. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/precursor-ion-metadata-extraction))
- **precursor-ion-mode-data-processing**: Use when AEMS plate data was acquired in full-scan precursor ion (PI) mode and all wells of a 384-well plate must be processed (peak detection, shot-to-peak association, area quantification) or benchmarked against the reported &lt;30 s runtime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-ion-mode-data-processing))
- **precursor-ion-overlap-analysis**: Use when you have a RegFilter-exported scheduled precursor list (.csv) and the matching MZmine MS1 feature table for the same LC-MS runs, and need to report the percentage of SPL ions present in MS1 and count SPL/DDA fragmented-precursor overlaps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-ion-overlap-analysis))
- **precursor-ion-peak-exclusion**: Use when preparing raw MS/MS peak lists for MMSA spectral alignment and uninformative peaks near the precursor ion (within ±17 m/z) must be excluded before top-6 intensity filtering and square-root transformation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-ion-peak-exclusion))
- **precursor-ion-peak-metrics-analysis**: Use when QC'ing a batch of LC-MS/LC-IMS-MS or infusion runs (Agilent '.d', Thermo '.raw', Bruker '.d', mzML) by profiling precursor-ion errors and flagging anomalous runs per metric and sample group rather than eyeballing spectra manually. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-ion-peak-metrics-analysis))
- **precursor-ion-tolerance-sweep-benchmarking**: Use when you need to characterize how runtime of a mass spectral search tool (e.g. VInSMoC) scales as precursor ion tolerance increases, or to compare exact vs. variable search cost head-to-head. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-ion-tolerance-sweep-benchmarking))
- **precursor-list-formatting-for-dda**: Use when you have generated a lipid spectral library (lipid identities, adducts, m/z values, fragmentation patterns) and your downstream analysis requires DDA acquisition on an Orbitrap instrument using Excalibur software. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-list-formatting-for-dda) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-m-z-based-filtering**: Use when you have an unknown MS/MS query spectrum with a known or measured precursor m/z value and need to search a spectral library (local or public: GNPS, MASSBANK, DrugBANK) to annotate the compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-m-z-based-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-mass-annotation**: Use when you hold a list of precursor accurate masses (e.g., oxPC precursor m/z values in a CSV) and need compound-level annotations — species name, molecular formula, expert-system score — from CMM 3.0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-annotation))
- **precursor-mass-calculation**: Use when when you have a compound's SMILES string or molecular formula and need to determine the expected precursor ion m/z for comparison against observed spectra, particularly before applying formula-based denoising, entropy similarity scoring, or denoising search against reference libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-mass-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-mass-candidate-filtering**: Use when searching query tandem mass spectra against large structure databases (e.g., PubChem, DNP) and you must first restrict candidate molecules by observed precursor mass before running fragment-matching scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-candidate-filtering))
- **precursor-mass-candidate-screening**: Use when a set of LC-MS precursor queries (measured m/z, optional RT) needs candidate structures from a database narrowed down, and orthogonal predicted properties (RT, CCS) are available to re-rank candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-candidate-screening))
- **precursor-mass-consistency-checking**: Use when you need to quantify how often adduct annotations in a mass spectral library (e.g., GNPS MGF spectra with SMILES) are wrong or can be corrected from SMILES plus precursor m/z alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-consistency-checking))
- **precursor-mass-defect-computation**: Use when MS/MS spectra of suspected chemically modified metabolites (methylation, acetylation, phosphorylation, hydroxylation) must be associated with library spectra of their non-modified parent compounds by measuring precursor and fragment mass shifts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-defect-computation))
- **precursor-mass-difference-alignment**: Use when comparing two tandem MS spectra (e.g. a library spectrum vs. a query spectrum of a related molecule) and you need to score match quality including peaks shifted by the precursor mass difference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-difference-alignment))
- **precursor-mass-difference-analysis**: Use when a large corpus of unidentified spectra has been clustered (e.g., GLEAMS clusters with medoid indexes) and you need to annotate representative spectra via open modification search and derive the mass-difference profile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-difference-analysis))
- **precursor-mass-difference-calculation**: Use when when comparing two MS/MS spectra using modified cosine similarity and the precursor m/z values differ, indicating potential neutral losses, adduct variations, or analogs with different substituents. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-mass-difference-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-mass-eligibility-filtering**: Use when assembling analog compound-pair benchmarks from MS/MS libraries — you have enumerated pairs and must enforce precursor mass and adduct eligibility before substructure matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-eligibility-filtering))
- **precursor-mass-feature-tracking**: Use when an unannotated molecular feature defined by precursor m/z (e.g., m/z 266.138, 280.153, 294.169) or an MS/MS spectrum must be searched across tissues or mapped from an animal model into public human metabolomics studies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-feature-tracking))
- **precursor-mass-filtering**: Use when after retrieving top-scoring library candidates from a full MS2Deepscore comparison, but before or during final re-ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-mass-filtering) · 3 copies)
- **precursor-mass-filtering-spectrum-search**: Use when matching EI-MS query spectra against a million-scale in-silico library and the query's molecular mass is known or estimable, so candidates can be restricted to a ±5 Da mass window to improve recall@1/recall@10. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-filtering-spectrum-search))
- **precursor-mass-neutral-loss-calculation**: Use when when analyzing tandem mass spectra (MS/MS data) and you want to incorporate neutral loss patterns—characteristic mass losses from molecular precursors—into your spectral similarity or feature representation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-mass-neutral-loss-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-mass-ppm-filtering**: Use when dereplicating untargeted LC-MS2 features (.mzML input) against public spectral libraries (GNPS, HMDB, MassBank) inside MAW-R and you need reproducible precursor-ppm and similarity-score filtering of candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-ppm-filtering))
- **precursor-mass-preselection-filtering**: Use when running modified cosine (or similar score-based) analogue searching against a large library (e.g., 302,514 GNPS spectra) and you need to bound pairwise score computations by precursor m/z proximity, or when benchmarking the runtime cost of such preselection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-preselection-filtering))
- **precursor-mass-shift-analog-detection**: Use when query MS/MS spectra from molecular networking or dereplication jobs show putative analogs of known compounds: the goal is to find library hits whose fragment peaks are systematically shifted by the precursor mass difference δ ± the user-defined fragment mass tolerance, rather than. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-shift-analog-detection))
- **precursor-mass-stratified-performance-analysis**: Use when benchmarking formula identification tools on tandem MS spectra and you need to quantify how accuracy degrades with increasing precursor m/z, especially for compounds above 800 Da. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-stratified-performance-analysis))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-alignment**: Use when untargeted LC-HRMS feature tables from a sample (e.g., AFFF) must be aligned to solvent-blank feature tables by precursor m/z to distinguish real sample features from background contamination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-alignment))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-benchmarking**: Use when benchmarking dereplication tools (DEREPLICATOR+, DEREPLICATOR-G, DEREPLICATOR) on spectra with known metabolite annotations and you need to measure how precursor mass tolerance settings (e.g. 0.5 Da vs 0.02 Da) change top-rank identification rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-benchmarking))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering**: Use when ranking candidate molecules for MS/MS spectra: precursor m/z must be used to restrict the 520,083-compound reference database before embedding similarity comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering--f9f662e389a4d0ac))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering**: Use when annotating LC–MS/MS metabolite peaks in plant metabolomics data and you need candidate metabolites filtered by MS1 precursor m/z tolerance (e.g., 25 ppm) prior to MS/MS and RT scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering--700d3727fb385ea5))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering**: Use when computing a sparse pairwise spectrum similarity matrix or nearest-neighbor candidate list and only pairs within a precursor mass tolerance (ppm or Dalton) are valid, as in MS/MS clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering--df20ed52326ac6ef))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering**: Use when after cosine similarity or dot-product scoring of experimental MS/MS spectra against a reference library, when you need to eliminate matches whose precursor m/z values deviate too far from the experimental precursor m/z, thereby improving specificity of library matching before final. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering**: Use when implementing de novo peptide prediction from MS/MS spectra and you need to constrain beam-search candidates to peptides consistent with the observed precursor m/z, accounting for isotope-error offsets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering--ebf7bba78725562d))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-matching**: Use when you have experimental MGF-format MS/MS spectra (e.g., human blood metabolomics) plus one or more reference MS/MS libraries, and you need to count how many spectra can be annotated by library search under cosine similarity thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-matching))
- **precursor-mass-window-filtering**: Use when annotating unassigned query MS/MS spectra against a reference library: before Denoising Search scoring, filter library entries whose precursor m/z or molecular formula mass lies outside the instrument's precursor mass accuracy window. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-window-filtering))
- **precursor-metadata-extraction-from-peak-lists**: Use when a peak list file in PKL (or a related text format like DTA, MS2, MGF) needs header parsing to recover precursor m/z, charge, and intensity fields, and per-spectrum records with peak lists must be assembled. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/precursor-metadata-extraction-from-peak-lists))
- **precursor-mz-candidate-filtering**: Use when matching unknown query MS/MS spectra against a large spectral library (e.g., ~76,000–95,000 AllPositive/GNPS spectra) and full pairwise scoring would be too slow: filter candidates by precursor m/z before computing similarity scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mz-candidate-filtering))
- **precursor-mz-candidate-retrieval**: Use when you have an MS/MS analyte precursor mass and need a putative compound candidate list from a metabolite database for annotation or fingerprint-score ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mz-candidate-retrieval))
- **precursor-mz-charge-filtering**: Use when dereplicating natural products by matching query MS/MS spectra against a reference spectral library and candidate library entries must be restricted by precursor charge state, precursor m/z tolerance, matched-peak count, and match score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mz-charge-filtering))
- **precursor-mz-charge-matching**: Use when confirming N-linked glycan identifications from LC-MS/MS mzXML data: an MS1 output Excel file lists candidate glycan-RT pairs, and the MS2 fragment scan for the matching precursor m/z and charge must be located for fragment annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mz-charge-matching))
- **precursor-mz-difference-histogramming**: Use when you hold two MGF peak-list files (e.g., serum tandem mass spectra from ProteomeXchange PXD034932) and want to visualize spectral similarity as a function of precursor m/z difference to inspect charge states, mass accuracy, and isotope errors without a database search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mz-difference-histogramming))
- **precursor-mz-mobility-charge-matching**: Use when detected isotope patterns from DDA-PASEF TDF/TIMS data must be validated against vendor-selected precursor lists, e.g. to measure how many of the N=6902 Bruker-selected peptides an LSH pipeline recovers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mz-mobility-charge-matching))
- **precursor-mz-pairing-with-ppm-tolerance**: Use when you need candidate spectrum pairs from an MS/MS library whose precursor m/z agree within ~10 ppm, to build same-vs-different-compound labels for AUROC evaluation of spectral similarity scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mz-pairing-with-ppm-tolerance))
- **precursor-mz-tolerance-based-retrieval**: Use when evaluating whether a spectral similarity metric (embeddings, supervised models, or 44 classical measures) retrieves the correct molecular structure from a spectral library, with candidates restricted by precursor m/z tolerance such as 10 ppm. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mz-tolerance-based-retrieval))
- **precursor-mz-tolerance-grouping**: Use when raw MS2 peak lists (.mzXML/.mzML/.mgf) from multiple LC-MS files must be collapsed into consensus spectra to build GNPS molecular network nodes, or when choosing a Minimum Cluster Size for a dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mz-tolerance-grouping))
- **precursor-mz-window-filtering**: Use when when preparing augmented training data for Siamese or contrastive learning architectures in mass spectrometry, specifically when you need to generate hard negative examples that are spectrally distinct but mass-similar to positive examples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-mz-window-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-peak-metric-extraction**: Use when raw MS files (Agilent 'd', Thermo '.raw', Bruker 'd', or mzML; LC-MS, LC-IMS-MS, DDA/DIA, direct infusion) need automated QC of precursor-ion peak metrics without identification-based scoring, e.g. batch-level metabolomics QC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-peak-metric-extraction))
- **precursor-peak-removal**: Use when a USI-loaded or MGF-loaded MS/MS spectrum (e.g. mzspec:PXD004732:...:scan:41840 or iPRG2012.mgf) still contains the precursor ion peak and must be cleaned before fragment annotation, noise filtering, or plotting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-peak-removal))
- **precursor-peak-removal-mass-tolerance**: Use when after loading an MsmsSpectrum object but before intensity filtering or spectral annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-peak-removal-mass-tolerance) · 3 copies)
- **precursor-peptide-extraction-from-clusters**: Use when you have assembled genome FASTA sequences (from SPAdes, metaSPAdes, or antiSMASH output) and need to systematically identify precursor peptides corresponding to a target RiPP class before constructing the structure database for dereplication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/precursor-peptide-extraction-from-clusters) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-product-ion-intensity-normalization**: Use when MS-DIAL result tables contain raw precursor and fragment ion intensities that must be converted to comparable 0–1 scales before rendering MS1×MS2 grayscale fingerprint images for CNN input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-product-ion-intensity-normalization))
- **precursor-product-mass-pairing**: Use when you have centroided MS2 spectra from data-dependent acquisition (ddMS2) in mzML format and seek to prioritize potential PFAS features by detecting diagnostic fragment masses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-product-mass-pairing) · 3 copies)
- **precursor-product-mz-parsing**: Use when you have raw MRM sample files from a LC-MS/MS instrument and need to systematically recover all precursor m/z and product m/z pairs for each transition.
